It is real, and its realness is His. ⇒ BOTTOM: The solidity of things is the signature of the One whose being is from Himself. ⇒ RETURN: Do not try to make the world vanish. Treat its solidity as His, and make it a home by deeds (stated; extends ON-14). - RB:RB-C03 [Why would the highest show up in the lowest place, and not in the spiritual?] (M1) You would expect the true Being to show in spiritual things, which are 'higher'. Why in physical things? → (M10) A big torch shows its strength only far away. Its faint light at a distance proves its power. → (M10) A barrel that overflows shows it is full by what spills outside, even though the spill is small. → (M10) Teaching passes an idea to a mind that already exists. Only a physical seed brings a new being into the world. → (M15) So His Essence shows in the physical deed, not only in spiritual work. A pillar holds the roof only when set in hard ground. → (M7) Even the heart's mitzvos must reach the flesh: fear that tightens the brain, study that adds wrinkles, delight that 'fattens the bones'. ⇒ BOTTOM: The highest is known only where it is farthest: in the hard, the physical, the done. ⇒ RETURN: Bring the insight into the body and into one physical deed today (stated). - RB:RB-C04 ['I, Havaya, have not changed.' But everything around us is change.] (M1) The light comes down world by world and gets smaller. Isn't that change in Him? → (M4) No: the differences come only from what each world can hold. The light itself is whole below as above. → (M11) And the receivers' weakness is from a covering, the name Elokim, which is itself holy. An essence does not hide an essence. → (M10) But every creature is different. Isn't that change? No: the word that makes each thing never leaves its source, like sunlight inside the sun. → (M16) Thinking this through makes a person want all his powers and wants to be only for G-d. → (M15) This you can do now. In the dark of exile the power of self-giving is even stronger than in Temple times. ⇒ BOTTOM: Nothing in Him ever changes; the hiding itself is holy. ⇒ RETURN: Offer your 'blood and fat', your life and your delight, to Him today; the rest He will reveal (stated). - RB:RB-C05 ['In your heart there is only One.' What does 'only One' add to total closeness?] (M1) The soul above was in total closeness to G-d. Why add that it had 'no foreign will'? → (M8) Speech serves thought like a chariot serves a rider: it obeys, but it is still a separate thing. → (M8) When you think the idea itself, 'thought within thought', the words are not even felt. They are swallowed in the idea. → (M5) 'Only One' means more than that. There is no existence besides Him, not even one that is nullified to Him. → (M7) This shines only at the innermost point of the heart. → (M12) The brothers, lower souls, could say it equally, because every soul's root is in the Essence, above even the will to create, where all are equal. → (M15) So a soul in a body full of foreign wants has the power to overcome them. ⇒ BOTTOM: Not 'everything bows to Him' but 'there is no second': not even a bowing one. ⇒ RETURN: That root is in you now, so the distraction in the body has no final hold (stated). - RB:RB-C06 [What, in a person, can actually reach G-d Himself?] (M1) Learning Torah builds your understanding, your 'self'. Return and good deeds are about yielding. Yet the sages call return and deeds the heart of Torah. → (M9) Thought cannot grasp Him. The heart's deep wish can, like a son who cries 'Abba!' and moves his father's whole being. → (M4) But even that essential love is still your will. Even wanting only His will is still a 'you' that wants. → (M5) Accepting His yoke, doing it because He said so, is not from your will. That is truer yielding. And even that does not grasp Him. → (M14) Only His own will can grasp Him. That is the mitzvah itself: it is His will. → (M12) To Him, yielding and being a self are exactly equal. Yielding is needed only so that what you draw down is seen, not hidden by your feeling of being the doer. → (M15) The truest yielding is in the deed done as a servant: you do not even feel that you are yielding; you feel only His will. ⇒ BOTTOM: He is grasped only by His own will: the commanded deed. ⇒ RETURN: Do one mitzvah today simply because He wants it, and let the feeling of 'I did it' go quiet (stated). - RB:RB-C07 [What is the one point of Chassidus, and where is it in me?] (M13) Chassidus has been described many ways: it woke a fainting world, it changes your nature, it makes the secrets plain. But it is one point, not a pile of qualities. → (M8) That point in a soul is the yechidah, the soul's essence. It is above all details, not even their 'source'. → (M9) We say Modeh Ani the moment we wake, before washing our hands. No impurity in the world can make a Jew's 'thank You' impure. → (M2) That is because the yechidah is always united with His Essence. A flaw cannot reach there. → (M5) That is why Modeh Ani has no Name of G-d in it: the soul's essence thanks the Essence that no name holds. → (M7) Reb Yekusiel did not want 'peasant years', years without seeing and hearing G-dliness. To him such years did not count as life at all. → (M12) The other levels of the soul have a self, so G-d is 'one more thing' for them. The yechidah leaves no room for anything but Him. ⇒ BOTTOM: The point of you that is always one with Him and cannot be stained. ⇒ RETURN: Start the day from it: Modeh Ani, before anything else, and stretch that dot across the whole day (the Rebbe Rashab as a child, line 54) (stated). - RB:RB-C08 [Everyone 'knows' the world is created each moment. Why don't we feel it?] (M4) Each morning you are not only given back your soul. Body and soul are made new, as at the first creation. → (M8) The soul's lower levels live inside the worlds, so what they feel plainly is that the worlds exist. Renewal is news to them. → (M5) Only the yechidah, above the worlds, plainly feels the worlds are nothing and are being made new each moment. → (M6) The power to turn nothing into something comes only from the Essence, which is above both 'nothing' and 'something'. → (M16) This is not to understand but to recognize, and recognition comes from the yechidah. → (M12) And the oneness does not erase the worlds. The worlds as they are, in time and place, are one with Him. → (M15) So when you do His command, no other aim may be mixed in, not even as a side aim. ⇒ BOTTOM: The yechidah's plain sense that all is being made now, by the One above 'is' and 'is not'. ⇒ RETURN: Waking each morning is a fresh creation; give thanks at once and do the day's first deed for Him alone (stated). - RB:RB-C09 [Does the deepest point of G-d sit apart from the small and the dark?] (M10) Oil is the essence of anything. Because it is essence, it is in everything. → (M12) In law too: oil does not mix with other liquids, and yet it soaks into everything. → (M4) A revelation is one particular thing, so it pushes away what is not it. An essence is the essence of each thing, so it pushes nothing away. → (M11) Lights can fight evil but cannot turn it into good, because evil is their opposite. → (M5) Only His Essence, utterly simple, with nothing opposite to it, can change evil into good. → (M15) So the lower Chassidus reaches, into the animal soul and into the world outside, the more its essence shows. → (M15) Your soul's essence wakes only when your powers work on something physical outside you, to use it for G-d. ⇒ BOTTOM: The Essence has no opposite, so it is in the lowest and can turn it. ⇒ RETURN: Whatever you learn, bring it to a deed with something physical today (stated). - RB:RB-C10 [If sin is against His will, how can it be part of His plan?] (M1) A fall that He willed must be for a rise. But a fall through sin is against His will. What proves it leads higher? → (M11) The Mitteler Rebbe: help from above can strengthen either side in a person, unfelt, so choice stays free. → (M6) The root of the very possibility of 'against His will' is the first contraction, when His light withdrew. → (M5) That contraction shows something: the Essence is not forced to reveal Himself. He can also be hidden. → (M9) Your power to choose, even to choose against Him, is His own ability, since He is forced by nothing. Serving Him by your own power reveals this. → (M12) The Baal Shem Tov read 'I do not know which He desires': there is a thought that His delight is even in the possibility of the wrong choice. But the verse ends 'the light is good'. → (M15) He is like a mirror of man. When you push against your nature, light comes down against its nature too. ⇒ BOTTOM: The hiddenness and the freedom are both from the Essence, which is compelled by nothing. ⇒ RETURN: Use your freedom: do the one thing today that goes against your habit (the 101st review, line 1397) (stated). - RB:RB-C11 ['You have been shown to know that Havaya is Elokim; there is none besides Him.' What is the bridge?] (M1) 'You' is the Essence, and the Essence is present everywhere. That is why everyone says G-d's name. → (M3) Havaya is revealing; Elokim is hiding. In themselves they are opposites. → (M5) They are one in the hidden sefiros and in the light before the contraction, but even there, in His will for worlds, they are still somewhat two. → (M5) They are truly one only in His ability: the ability to shine and the ability not to shine are the same ability. → (M9) You see it in yourself: we love G-d with both inclinations. Even the evil inclination, rooted in hiding, can love Him. → (M14) And the ability is nothing besides the Essence. It is only that He is able. That is 'there is none besides Him'. → (M15) Only when you reach that ability does darkness itself turn into light. ⇒ BOTTOM: His ability, in which showing and hiding are one. ⇒ RETURN: Do not only add light; take the dark thing itself and turn it, and dance with the feet that lift the head (stated). - RB:RB-C12 ['I take the King.' Why is choosing G-d called clever, and what does it rule out?] (M1) A king enters with dukes and generals. One says 'I take the duke'; the clever one says 'I take the king'. But even a child knows the king is greater. Why clever? → (M2) First answer: idol-worshippers thought the stars choose what to give. Jews know they are only an axe in the woodcutter's hand. → (M4) But in the parable the dukes do have choice. So the clever one prefers serving the king to getting more. The flow from the other side comes free and big; Jews choose the smaller, holy portion. → (M4) Not because holiness pays more later. What goes to those who defy Him is thrown over His shoulder to an enemy; what goes to those who do His will comes from His inner will. → (M8) Still, that is reasoning, and the mind on its own wants what is good for itself. The choice comes from the soul, above the mind, and then teaches the mind. → (M10) A palace has rooms full of treasure. Some stop in the first room, some in an inner one. The wise one is not held even by the inner treasure; he wants only the room where the king is. → (M5) So the Alter Rebbe: I want nothing; not Your Garden of Eden, not Your World to Come; I want only You. → (M11) Idolatry begins when a means is treated as the goal. Wanting even Gan Eden for yourself can, through a long chain, end there. → (M16) Every Jew can wake at least a trace of 'only You'. ⇒ BOTTOM: Him alone, not even His gifts. ⇒ RETURN: Let your many wants be 'without heart'; let the main want be His home, and use what He gives to make it (stated; line 1905). - RB:RB-C13 [Light shows, a name calls. Which reaches the self?] (M1) He is One beyond any count, and Israel are one of a kind. So why 'One' (echad), which can be counted, and not 'Single' (yachid)? → (M3) A name does not tell you who a person is. But it draws the person himself. → (M9) Call someone by name and he turns to you with all of himself. → (M2) Even someone who has fainted wakes when called by his name: the name reaches the core of life, beneath the life that has gone out of the limbs. → (M12) 'One' says the worlds are here and yet one with Him. The highest is that they stay real and are still utterly nullified. → (M5) Self-giving for G-d's Name is the utmost yielding, and it draws the Essence's holiness into the Name. → (M15) Remembering that readiness brings the soul's essence into each detail of your day. ⇒ BOTTOM: A name reaches the self beneath every spreading of life. ⇒ RETURN: Say His Name as one who is called by name; let it wake you from the faint (ours: the application; the image is stated). - RB:RB-C14 ['As water reflects a face.' How can my small face appear in the Infinite?] (M9) As water shows the face that looks into it, so one heart answers another, and so the upper Man answers the lower man. → (M1) Water reflects because it has no colour. But it can reflect only things of its own kind. The Infinite is beyond even the existence of sefiros. How can a form appear in it? → (M14) Only because He willed it. Once He willed it, the water and the face became of one kind. → (M2) And He willed it because Israel's root is in His Essence, as a son's image is engraved in his father's mind. → (M10) Those 'hidden sefiros' are only an ability, like a person's name. A name only means: if called, he will turn. → (M4) The face seen in the water is not a separate thing in the water, like a fish. It is the water itself. → (M15) Refining the animal soul thins the curtains between the levels until the face below shows the water above. ⇒ BOTTOM: Your face in Him is not a picture beside Him; it is Him. ⇒ RETURN: Look in, and work on one trait today so the reflection is clear (ours: the wording; stated: the teaching). - RB:RB-C15 [If I truly saw that He is all, would I disappear?] (M10) Sea creatures are covered by the sea and feel their source; on land they die. Land creatures grow on the surface and feel separate. → (M1) Tanya says that if we saw the life in things, they would vanish; elsewhere it says the same life gives them existence. Which is it? → (M4) Nullified means covered like fish in the sea, not gone. → (M1) Then real contemplation of 'G-d is One' should make the soul expire, like Aaron's sons. → (M5) It doesn't, because the Essence wants a home below, and to Him sea and land are equal. The body itself is rooted in the true Being. → (M10) The mitzvos are the ships that let a land creature cross the sea without drowning. → (M12) At the Sea, Israel walked on dry land inside the sea and were not undone by the light. That second kindness was greater than the splitting. → (M15) Other prophets fell when the vision came. Moses stood whole. At the Sea even a maidservant pointed and said 'This is my G-d'. ⇒ BOTTOM: The Essence, to whom sea and land are equal, keeps you standing on dry ground inside the sea. ⇒ RETURN: Do not seek to dissolve. Cross on the ship of a mitzvah, and stand (stated). - RB:RB-C16 [Two kinds of faith, and a brokenness higher than self-sacrifice.] (M8) Jews believe without proofs for two reasons: the soul above sees G-d, and the soul's essence is bound to Him. → (M4) Faith from 'seeing' is strong, but it has a cause, so it is not tied to your very being. → (M5) Faith from the essence is the person himself. Therefore he simply cannot deny it. → (M7) Even in comfort, a Jew can be crushed that we are in exile, because a revealed G-d touches his very being. → (M4) Even if he himself has great light: as long as one corner of the world lacks it, his light too is limited. → (M5) The Alter Rebbe was often heard saying: I want nothing at all, only You. That gave every Jew the power to want this. → (M4) This brokenness is higher than self-sacrifice. People who gave their lives for years under decrees often lose that fire in freedom: the essence had not changed their everyday powers. → (M15) The lamp is 'always' only when your everyday powers become one with your essence. ⇒ BOTTOM: Faith that is the self, and an ache for Him that is the self. ⇒ RETURN: Ask three times a day 'may our eyes see' and mean it; let the ache shape your ordinary hours (stated). - RB:RB-C17 [Self-sacrifice: sought, by-the-way, always ready, and grabbed.] (M7) Rabbi Akiva longed for the chance to give his life. Abraham did not look for it; it came 'by the way' while he made others call G-d's name. → (M2) The higher kind is not for the beauty of self-sacrifice. It is what you do because there is no other way to do your task. → (M5) There is the self-sacrifice the law requires, and the one that is always ready, even when not required, from the Jew's place above the Torah itself. → (M9) A mohel not ready to 'break' Shabbos to circumcise a child cannot circumcise even on a weekday, since circumcision brings in that bond above the Torah. → (M16) Keeping the mitzvos depends on remembering your self-sacrifice, so Moses had Shema said twice daily even when safety was promised. → (M7) The Rebbe Rayatz in Soviet Russia: 'Jews, grab self-sacrifice! Soon there will be freedom, and you will look for a way to give yourselves and not find it.' → (M15) In freedom, grab every mitzvah, without calculations, obstacles, or fear of mockers. ⇒ BOTTOM: The bond above the Torah's own measure, which every Jew carries. ⇒ RETURN: In Shema each day, say it ready; then grab one mitzvah you were going to postpone (stated). - RB:RB-C18 [Why is the plain way the deepest?] (M7) The Baal Shem Tov worked to have simple people say 'Baruch Hashem'. When a simple Jew says it, he means the Essence. → (M15) A simple Jew does a mitzvah because he was commanded, not knowing what it does above. → (M5) After all the understanding of what mitzvos do above, the height is to do them plainly. The end goes back into the beginning. → (M2) The plainness of a Jew is one with the simplicity of the Essence. → (M10) The prince far from home forgot even his language and cried out in a plain voice. The forgetting is a virtue: words limit. → (M4) Even the deepest intention can dim the strength of simple acceptance. → (M12) There are two poverties. At first you believe because you do not understand; at the end, like Moses, all is faith, because before Him every mind is simple. ⇒ BOTTOM: Plainness: the human trace of His utter simplicity. ⇒ RETURN: Say 'Baruch Hashem' today meaning Him alone; do one mitzvah with no story attached (stated). - RB:RB-C19 [The four letters of G-d's name, as four movements in a person.] (M8) Yud: you lay yourself down before G-d entirely, not reckoning with your mind. → (M4) Not even because your mind agrees that some things are above the mind. With no calculations at all. → (M5) First Hei: you see that every revelation, even the highest, is a small glimmer after many contractions, and you are far. It becomes so narrow you find no place. → (M3) For that to sink in truly, the Yud must come first, because the mind is itself a 'something' and cannot by itself feel that all is far. → (M7) Vav: from the narrowness you cry out, and the cry brings you to Torah. → (M15) Final Hei: the deed. The will in a physical deed is not dressed in anything, because the deed is beyond comparison. → (M15) Doing mitzvos with simple faith even opens the taste of Torah: 'teach me good taste and knowledge, for I believed in Your mitzvos'. ⇒ BOTTOM: A self laid down with no calculation, and the narrow place that cries. ⇒ RETURN: Before prayer: lay yourself down (yud), feel the narrowness (hei), cry (vav), then do (hei) (ours: arranged as practice; each step stated). - RB:RB-C20 [The prayer of a rich man: what can the one who lacks nothing ask?] (M1) Moses' prayer is 'the prayer of a rich man'. But prayer asks to fill a lack, and the rich lack nothing. → (M2) Rich means rich in himself. Someone with much that he only received is still poor. → (M8) Understanding always leaves room for the opposite view. Recognition, felt knowing, does not. → (M5) Even felt knowing of the lower kind still leaves room for something outside G-d. → (M5) The upper knowing comes from His Essence, and there the truth of His being is the truth of all beings. Knowing G-d becomes your whole existence. → (M12) So for Moses even Israel's lack was not a lack. He prayed that they too would feel that, in truth, they lack nothing. → (M15) 'Man of G-d': going up in prayer, remember you are a man with traits to refine; coming down, be 'Elokim', strong enough to judge yourself all day. ⇒ BOTTOM: Where the truth of His being is the truth of all beings, nothing is missing. ⇒ RETURN: Pray from fullness for someone else's lack; then let the prayer judge your day (stated). - RB:RB-C21 [How can return make a new person, and why can anyone return?] (M2) Return means: 'I am someone else, not the man who did those things'. → (M4) For a righteous person, return is implicit in his will. For one who truly sinned, it is not a continuation of anything before. It is new. → (M5) It draws a light that was not even in the plan of creation, from the soul's root above that plan. → (M11) Why does a sinner come back at all? Because the point of Jewishness is always whole; even during the sin the soul is faithful to Him. → (M12) Yom Kippur's very day reveals the level where there was no flaw to begin with. → (M13) At that level all are equal. → (M15) Return is a leap, out of order: 'before they call, I will answer'. ⇒ BOTTOM: A bond that was never broken, revealed by a leap. ⇒ RETURN: Return now, without waiting to become worthy first (stated). - RB:RB-C22 [Can the war inside me end in peace?] (M1) War is called 'krav', closeness, because to defeat someone you must come close. The G-dly soul fights by dressing itself in the animal soul's terms. → (M4) A stronger love, from the soul's higher level, can silence the animal's wants. But that is only one will overpowering another. Not real peace. → (M5) The yechidah has no opposite at all. From there, there was never room for foreign wants. → (M16) Even someone low, whose animal soul is fighting him, can have this: by thinking about the yechidah and deciding to live by it. → (M9) A scholar told the Tzemach Tzedek, 'they step on me in the study hall'. He answered: 'You have spread yourself over the whole study hall, so wherever anyone steps, it is on you.' → (M11) Leave even the finest thoughts of self, even the finest of the fine. → (M11) The sparks in the world want to be raised. From the yechidah you see the world helping you, not blocking you. ⇒ BOTTOM: The point with no opposite, where the war never began. ⇒ RETURN: In a fight with yourself, stop arguing; think of the yechidah and decide; and shrink the self that gets stepped on (stated). - RB:RB-C23 [When the Essence shows, a command becomes unnecessary.] (M3) Prayer asks and may not be answered. A blessing commands, and so it happens, but it only gives what is already in the source. → (M10) Rabbi Pinchas commanded a river to split. Rabbi Shimon only taught Torah, gave no order at all, and the rain came. → (M2) A command is needed only when the one commanded is a separate thing. → (M5) When the Essence is revealed, Whose being is the being of all, the rain falls by itself. → (M7) In prayer you feel that you are not G-dliness. → (M15) Any part of you that is not G-dliness blocks the revealing. So a chassid's natural powers must become G-dly powers. → (M15) A chassid serves for G-d's need, not even for his own closeness to G-d. ⇒ BOTTOM: The truth of His being is the being of all, so there is no 'other' to command. ⇒ RETURN: Serve for His sake, not for your spiritual gain, and let your natural powers be His (stated). - RB:RB-C24 [Where does the deepest cry come out, and what does G-d hold most precious?] (M7) The inner cry from the depth of the heart comes out only through the 'stone' in a person: plain acceptance, and the physical deed. → (M15) So the shofar must be an animal's horn cut off, a lifeless thing. → (M5) The Alter Rebbe: 'You have no idea how precious to G-d the body of a Jew is.' → (M4) It does not mean 'even the body'. The main preciousness is in the body. It is beyond grasp because it comes from the Essence. → (M12) The covenant was cut by passing between the pieces of one animal: two who become one flesh. His union with Israel includes the physical body. → (M16) 'G-d will return with your captivity': wherever a Jew is, even at the edge of the heavens, His very Essence is there with him. ⇒ BOTTOM: The body, chosen by the Essence, is where the Essence holds most. ⇒ RETURN: Do the plain physical deed as the place of your deepest cry; do not despise the body (stated). - RB:RB-C25 [A home in the lowest: does the lowest have to stop being low?] (M10) A road joins two places far apart, the king's city and the farthest corner. → (M10) A home in the lowest is like a man living in his friend's house with all of himself, as in his own. → (M12) The house stays the friend's house. The lowest stays low, and the Essence still lives in it. → (M4) A road must go back too. If you cannot return on it, it is not a road, even from where you started. → (M5) Real drawing-down soaks into every part of the place until it becomes one with it. → (M15) So give charity with judgment: judge yourself out of luxuries, keep what you truly need, and give the rest. ⇒ BOTTOM: The Essence at home in a house that stays the lower's house. ⇒ RETURN: Judge your luxuries today and give; let the road run both ways (stated). - RB:RB-C26 [What does a commanded deed testify to?] (M7) After Sinai the deed itself is the main thing. Intend the secrets of matzah without eating, and you draw nothing; eat without intention, and you draw the light. → (M3) The surrounding light cannot be grasped, only known by negation: knowing that it is beyond knowing. → (M10) But to say G-d 'cannot be grasped' is like saying a deep idea cannot be touched with the hands. Anyone would laugh. → (M5) So even 'beyond the mind' does not apply to His Essence. Mitzvos reach Him only because they are His will. → (M9) 'You are My witnesses': a Jew's very existence testifies to the Essence. → (M16) The wise son's Torah is new every day, so he always stands before Sinai again, like a child just born. ⇒ BOTTOM: The Essence, whom not even 'unknowable' describes, testified by a deed and by your being. ⇒ RETURN: Do the deed even when the meaning is gone; start again each day as one newly born (stated).

## RK — the Rashab's kuntreisim

The Rebbe Rashab's short works add the inside of the climb: how a person actually gets from knowing 'nothing but Him' to living it, and why it so often fails. Five findings. (1) The hiding is His. Nature is not water over a sunk thing; He made the cover, so G-d in nature is G-d beyond nature, and solidity itself is made of His hiding, which is His kindness (Umaayan 23-25). (2) Near is quiet. Fire-love cries 'Abba' from a distance; when one feels that nothing hides Him and time and space are not real limits, love becomes water: full, still, whispered, as in the Amidah (Eitz HaChaim 2). (3) Reach above your level. The lower unity alone slips into 'somethingness'; everyone must touch the upper unity a little, and it is true for that hour (Eitz HaChaim 7-10). (4) The essence moves only through the narrow place. Love is the soul's own pull, like a son's, not a product of thinking; but great arousals change nothing unless the pain reaches the animal soul itself and daily work prepared the ground (HaAvodah 5). Fear, the plain 'one may not', touches the Essence; love touches lights (HaAvodah 3). (5) Under reason is a spring. Where you are stuck, don't argue; act from the soul's stubborn strength, which outlasts reason, and understanding returns (Umaayan 26-28). Toras Shalom in this file is the Rebbe Rashab's halachic responsa; it adds little to depth beyond one line: before the Essence wisdom and speech are equal (OC 4).

## Depths

- **RK:RK-D01 Nature is the beyond-nature** (extends D10 (sharpens: the world is given back not only as kept, but as itself beyond nature)): Nature does not hide G-d the way water hides a sunk thing. He made the cover and lives inside it, so the G-d in nature is the very G-d above nature. What keeps us from seeing it is only habit. (stated, Umaayan 25) | Inside: The text's image is the drowned thing that is there all along, and 'open G-dliness' (Yiddish, offene Getlichkeit) that needs no thinking (ours: the inside feels like a familiar room suddenly lit). | Lands: In the workday: effort is a garment; the blessing is His. Work, but don't bow your head to the work (U525-536). | Q: האלקות המלובש בטבע הוא עצמו האלקות שלמעלה מהטבע — the G-dliness clothed in nature is itself the G-dliness above nature (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres Umaayan (Lubavitch, 1903).txt:519)
- **RK:RK-D02 Solid because hidden, hidden because loved** (extends D10 (deepens: the world's solidity is His signature because it is His hiding, and the hiding is His love)): Every 'something' is made by hiding: open light leaves no existence, and the more hiding, the more solid a thing feels. The hiding is not distance but kindness, like a father who plays with his son using nutshells. (stated, Umaayan 23-24) | Inside: A father making himself small; a child happy with the shell, not knowing the fruit (U499). | Lands: Every physical good (bread, health, children) is the higher kindness dressed down to reach you; use it for Torah and mitzvos (U500-503). | Q: דכאשר האור מאיר בגילוי אז אין שום מציאות — when the light shines openly there is no existence (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres Umaayan (Lubavitch, 1903).txt:483)
- **RK:RK-D03 Near the Father, the cry goes quiet** (extends D2 and D13 (extends: the deeper stillness is not only given, it is the shape love takes when nearness is felt; silence is fuller than fire)): Love that burns and cries 'Abba, Abba' belongs to one who is still a little far. When you truly feel that nothing hides Him and that even time and space are not real limits, the burning stops and the heart is full and silent. (stated, Eitz HaChaim 2) | Inside: The son beside his father: 'its inside lined with love', nothing shown (E150). | Lands: The whispered Amidah and its bows; then Torah study of practical law to keep the stillness nailed in all day (E150-153). | Q: אבל באמת אין שום דבר חמעלים ומפתיר כלל לגבי' ית' — but in truth nothing at all hides or conceals before Him (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres Eitz HaChaim (scans, OCR).txt:150)
- **RK:RK-D04 True for this hour** (extends D1 and D14 (new emphasis: permission and duty to touch the highest level even when it is not yours; corrects 'that's not for me')): You may not live at the level of the upper unity, where everything is nullified in Him. Still you must reach for it a little, because the lower level alone slips. And what you reach is not pretend: it is true in that hour, and again whenever you remember. (stated, Eitz HaChaim 7-9) | Inside: A whisper: the king's daughter's servants bring the sharp dish 'in a whisper' (E242). | Lands: At meals: with even a little of this, you can taste the food and it does not drag you down (E227). | Q: לבוא מעט מזעיר עכ"פ ואפילו בדרך דמיון — to come to it at least a tiny bit, even as if imagining (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres Eitz HaChaim (scans, OCR).txt:212)
- **RK:RK-D05 The son's pull** (extends D6 and D8 (extends: under all names a point never apart, and that point is a living pull, a son's longing, not a static point)): Love of G-d is not made by thinking. Under the love that thinking stirs, the soul has a will that pulls to its Source like everything pulls to its root, with no reason. It runs even though it is always one with Him, because it came out like a son from a father. Thinking only uncovers it. (stated, Kuntres HaAvodah 5) | Inside: Father and son, whose love is never hidden (A172); the soul 'held and attached to You' (A163). | Lands: In plain wholeness of deed and in each test: the 'oath' gives strength from the essence (A164). | Q: כמו טבע כל דבר שנמשך לשרשו ומקורו כך הוא: טבע הנשמה לימשך ולהכלל בשרשה ומקורה — as everything is drawn to its root, so the soul's nature is to be drawn and included in its root (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres HaAvodah (Lubavitch, 1911).txt:163)
- **RK:RK-D06 The narrow place is the door** (extends D9 (sharpens the turn: the bottom that sends you back is the narrow place, and it transforms only where daily work prepared it)): High arousals (wonder, Yom Kippur tears) can be true and still change nothing. The essence of the soul breaks out and changes a person when it comes from the narrow place, when even the animal soul hurts and 'has no place in itself', and only on top of daily work. (stated, Kuntres HaAvodah 5) | Inside: 'He has no place at all in himself'; 'like one fleeing from death to life' (A182). | Lands: Choose the one trait you must refine; work on it all year, so the great days have somewhere to land (A193-197). | Q: ומזה נעשה היציאה בבחי' תוקף הרצוא שלמעלח מטו"ד — from this comes the breakout, with the force of running above reason (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres HaAvodah (Lubavitch, 1911).txt:178)
- **RK:RK-D07 'But one may not' touches the Essence** (extends D7 and D13 (extends: simple faith and plainness are not only above thought; the plain 'may not' is the Essence felt in hiding)): The plainest fear, 'one simply may not', some have from birth with no effort. It is a hidden sense of the Essence itself. Love belongs to lights and revelations; fear, even at its lowest, to the Essence. (stated, Kuntres HaAvodah 3) | Inside: 'Mi'n tor dokh nit', but one may not (A105). | Lands: Constant remembering through the day; guarding eyes and ears flows from it by itself (A76-78, A101). | Q: מען טטר דאך ניט — but one may not! (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres HaAvodah (Lubavitch, 1911).txt:105)
- **RK:RK-D08 Holy stubbornness** (extends D7 (extends: what thought can't hold, the will holds; here the will acts without thought and outlasts it)): Below reason is folly that leads to sin. Above reason is holy folly: the soul's strength to do good with no reason and no debate. The stubborn will is stronger, because reason limits will. The one who is not shining should not negotiate with the yetzer at all. (stated, Umaayan 26-28) | Inside: A spring dripping drop by drop, never stopping, beside a wide river that dries (U571); the 'stiff neck' as virtue (U565). | Lands: In the one sunken habit, act the opposite without debate; in business and prayer times, don't calculate gain and loss (U568, U585). | Q: והיינו שאינו נכנס כלל בחשבונות על פי הדעת והשגה שצריך להיות כך וכך אלא עושה כל הדברים מצד תוקף הנפש שלמעלה מטעם ודעת — he does not enter calculations of what must be so and so, but does everything from the soul's strength above reason (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres Umaayan (Lubavitch, 1903).txt:556)
- **RK:RK-D09 Thirst is born below** (extends D9 and D14 (extends: no bottom, because the thirst itself is born of the descent and is never closed here)): The soul above had no thirst, because it had no opposite and no work. Thirst for G-d is born from coming down into a body, and here it cannot be quenched, because here we know only that He is, not what He is. (stated, Umaayan 6) | Inside: Fire that always rises and falls on the wick (U92); the moon's 'heh' that thirsts (U93). | Lands: Eat and work for heaven's sake; the joy above ('G-d made laughter for me') comes from service down here (U108-109). | Q: שהצמאון הזה גם כן לא היה בהנשמה קודם, אלא דוקא על ידי ירידתה בגוף — this thirst was not in the soul before, but only through her descent into the body (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres Umaayan (Lubavitch, 1903).txt:116)
- **RK:RK-D10 The moisture of the deep (Rebbe Rayatz, letter of 1931 printed with Kuntres HaAvodah)** (extends D8 (extends: the bond you cannot let go works silently, by presence)): There is a love that does nothing and still works: like the deep springs that never come to the surface, yet because they are there the earth is moist and things can grow. The eternal love works by being there, not by acting. (stated by the Rebbe Rayatz, in the letter appended to Kuntres HaAvodah; not the Rebbe Rashab's own text) | Inside: 'Feichtkeit', moisture of the earth (A280). | Lands: Plant mitzvos in the moist ground; the growth comes from the deep (ours, from A280). | Q: מעינות תהום רבה הנוזלים עמוק עמוק בבטן האדמה — the springs of the great deep that flow far down in the belly of the earth (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres HaAvodah (Lubavitch, 1911).txt:280)
- **RK:RK-D11 Strip the picture, keep a thin veil** (extends D5 (sharpens: the end of knowing is reached by stripping pictures, and it ends in a bow, not a blank)): You come to a G-dly matter through examples and details; then you put them down and come to the thing itself, which has no such shape. But some thin veil always stays. At the top you know Him by what He is not, and you bow. (stated, Kuntres HaAvodah 1) | Inside: The natural senses falling quiet (A40); taking a G-dly matter 'altogether differently' (A53). | Lands: The bow of the Amidah (A53); then everyday understanding changes too. | Q: ואח"כ שולל ומפשיט ומניח את הענינים שבהם הוא משיג את הענין ובא אל עצם הדבר המושג — then he negates, strips and sets aside what he grasped it through, and comes to the thing itself (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres HaAvodah (Lubavitch, 1911).txt:42)
- **RK:RK-D12 Feeling is knowing** (extends new (a missing step between 'knowing nothing but Him' and change: knowing must become feeling to move the will)): Daas is not information; it is feeling the thing. A gifted child knows everything and feels nothing; a cruel person knows the poor man's pain and doesn't feel it. Feeling G-d's height is the same act as feeling the lowness of the bad, and only that gives real choice. (stated, Kuntres HaTefillah 5-9) | Inside: 'Add knowledge, add pain' (T78): feeling hurts and heals. | Lands: Many prayers aimed at one trait until it bends (T104). | Q: וכמו תינוק חכים שמבין הכל ומ"מ אינו מרגיש עצם הענין — like a clever child who understands everything yet does not feel the thing itself (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres HaTefillah (Lubavitch, 1900).txt:61)
- **RK:RK-D13 The highest fell furthest** (extends D12 (extends: the deepest lands lowest because it fell from highest)): A stone from the top of a wall lands furthest away. Man got the coarsest body because his soul is the highest, and only it can refine the coarsest stuff. So a person can be worse than a beast and higher than an angel. (stated, Umaayan 15) | Inside: Stones falling from a wall (U305). | Lands: The body and its appetites are the material (U309); refine them in eating and dealing (U108). | Q: וכל אבן שהיא גבוה גבוה יותר הנה כשנופלת היא בריחוק מן הכותל ביותר — the higher the stone, the further from the wall it lands (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Kuntres Umaayan (Lubavitch, 1903).txt:305)
- **RK:RK-D14 Before the Essence, high and low are equal** (extends D13 (extends: plainness is nearest because before the Essence the high is as low as the low)): Before the Essence, wisdom and speech are the same, and wisdom is as 'physical' as deed. Hiding and revealing don't apply to Him. So the simple Jew who says Tehillim without understanding, in pure acceptance of the yoke, stands as near as the scholar, or nearer. (stated, Toras Shalom OC 4; Kuntres HaTefillah 11; Umaayan 15) | Inside: The simple Jew, ashamed, still singing his Tehillim (U322). | Lands: Plain prayer and plain deed; no credit taken (U331-336). | Q: דלגבי עצמות אוא"ס שלמעלה מסדר השתלשלות הרי החכמה והדבור שוין — before the Essence of the Infinite Light, beyond the order of worlds, wisdom and speech are equal (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Toras Shalom (Lubavitch, 1898-1919).txt:81)

## Chains (start → steps → bottom → return)

- RK:RK-C01 [G-d is hidden in nature.] (M12) There is G-d hidden in nature and G-d out in the open. The difference is only for us. For G-d it makes no difference at all whether He wears nature's clothes or not. → (M3) The word for nature, teva, means 'sunk', as in 'they sank in the Red Sea'. A thing sunk in the sea is covered by water so you cannot tell it is there. → (M4) But covering is not erasing. The sunk thing is fully there, as real as if it lay in the open. → (M10) Then the text breaks its own parable. The sea did not make the sunk thing, and the sunk thing did not make the sea. G-d made the very clothes that cover Him, and He keeps them alive. → (M12) So the G-d dressed in nature is the very same G-d who is above nature. Nature itself is beyond nature. → (M16) Why don't we feel this? Habit. The sun rises in the east and sets in the west every day, so it stops moving us. Only slow, deep thinking shows that nature itself is a G-dly thing. → (M15) Now it lands in your workday. Your business is only a garment, a messenger. If your whole mind goes into the deal and pushes out prayer and Torah, you have bowed your head to the garment. ⇒ BOTTOM: Nature itself is G-d beyond nature, only covered, and the cover is His own (stated, U519-520). ⇒ RETURN: Make a vessel for livelihood, but a small one; keep prayer and fixed learning first; know who gives through the garment (stated, U525-541). - RK:RK-C02 [Why does good come to us through nature and the stars?] (M14) For anything to become physical, the light must be hidden, layer under layer. Every 'something' is made by hiding. → (M4) When light shines openly there is no 'something' at all. Open light cancels existence. → (M13) So the more hidden, the more solid. How real a thing feels is a measure of how much is covering it. → (M11) The laws of nature and the stars are the thickest cover, like sackcloth. They hide so well that people mistake the garment for the giver. → (M11) But the hiding itself is His kindness. Without it we could not receive a good that fits our size. → (M9) A father who wants to play with his small son makes himself small and plays with nuts, even with the empty shells. The child does not know the fruit inside; to him the shell is the kindness. → (M15) So don't praise the tool. The stars are an axe in the builder's hand; no one admires the axe for the building. ⇒ BOTTOM: The world's solidness is His hiding, and His hiding is His kindness (stated, U481-508). ⇒ RETURN: Use the physical good for Torah and mitzvos, which is why it was given (stated, U500-503); in every good thing, look past the tool to the hand. - RK:RK-C03 [All the worlds are only a glimmer of Him.] (M5) Start from the distance: all worlds, with all their space and time, are only a glimmer, with no comparison to His Essence. → (M6) Even space is held inside Him: 'here is a place with Me'. Place is nullified to Him; He is not in place. → (M7) From that distance a fire is lit: 'Whom do I have in heaven? With You I want nothing on earth', neither spiritual nor physical pleasure. → (M4) But in truth, time and space down here are not real limits at all. → (M3) Proof: in the Temple the place of the Ark took up no space; and Adam was shown every generation in a short time. That is how the whole world truly is. → (M4) There is truly nothing that hides from Him. The Infinite shines openly even here below. → (M16) When you feel that nearness, you don't burn or thirst. You become still. This is love like water: it cools the fire. → (M9) A son who is still a little far from his father cries 'Abba, Abba!' with great feeling and thirst. → (M10) Right next to his father, his heart is full of love, but nothing shows outside the heart. → (M15) That is why the Amidah is whispered. In Pesukei d'Zimra you are still far and burning; in the Amidah you stand before the King, say 'Blessed are You', bow, and go quiet. → (M15) After the Amidah you go back to how you were. The fix is Torah study, even the laws about plain physical things, which keeps that stillness nailed in all day. ⇒ BOTTOM: Nothing hides Him, so nearness is silent: the cry of 'Abba' gives way to a full, quiet heart (stated, E150). ⇒ RETURN: Say the Amidah as a whisper before the King; then learn practical law so the stillness stays all day (stated, E150-153). - RK:RK-C04 [G-d hides Himself in the world through the name Elokim.] (M4) The name that hides, Elokim, does not truly hide the name that creates. It hides only from the creatures' side. → (M6) So the worlds are nullified in being, like the sun's rays inside the sun, which don't count as anything there. → (M12) Exactly as they were one with Him before they were created, so they are one with Him now. → (M16) Think it through deeply until it is truly felt in you. Then foreign wants simply fall away. → (M8) The lower way: you cannot feel the force that makes you. If you felt it, you would not exist as a 'something' at all. → (M9) Still, you can understand it from your own body: 'from my flesh I see G-d'. That understanding makes you want the G-dliness in each thing, not the thing. ⇒ BOTTOM: Upper unity: the hider does not hide, so the world is like rays in the sun. Lower unity: the hiding is real for us, and we serve inside it (stated, E199-207). ⇒ RETURN: In the lower way your deeds stay physical but aimed at heaven; in the upper way even your physical deeds are not physical, but G-dliness (stated, E207). - RK:RK-C05 [Most people today serve at the lower level of unity.] (M1) Question: most souls now serve at the lower level. So why must everyone reach for the upper unity, even a little, even almost like pretending? Doesn't each person have to know his place? → (M11) Because the lower level alone slips. It works with the force dressed inside the world, which also feeds the other side, so it is easy to fall and become a full 'something'. → (M16) If you understand it well, in that hour your heart truly wants that oneness. The hidden love in every Jew helps you. → (M12) It is not the full truth for you, and yet it is true in that hour, and again each time you remember. → (M15) With even a little of it, you can still taste the food, and the pleasure does not drag you down. → (M12) But don't skip the lower like the men of Jericho. The refining of body and animal soul happens only at the lower level, and its root is higher. → (M10) Parable: the king's daughter smelled the scrapings at the bottom of the pot, sharp and spiced. Say it shames her; don't say it pains her. Her servants bring it to her in a whisper: the whisper is the upper stillness inside the lower work. → (M12) Both together only are good. → (M14) Adam's sin was holding the moon without the sun: the lower level cut off from the upper. → (M15) His repair was sewing fig leaves: binding the tree of knowledge back to the tree of life. ⇒ BOTTOM: Even a little of the upper oneness, true only for an hour, is what keeps the lower work from falling (stated, E214-225). ⇒ RETURN: Each Shema, accept His oneness as real for you now (E224); then do the refining work of the day with a whisper of that stillness inside it (E242). - RK:RK-C06 [Love for G-d comes from thinking about Him.] (M2) The word for love, ahava, comes from 'avah', will. Will is deeper than feelings and can even change them: one who fasts on Shabbos because fasting is his pleasure does not feel the hunger as pain. → (M8) Souls have a natural will that runs to the Infinite, in Yiddish 'a tzi tzu', a pull toward. → (M6) Like everything is drawn to its root, the soul is drawn to be included in its source, without any reason or arousal. → (M1) But if the soul is always truly one with Him, 'held and attached to You', why does it run at all? → (M12) Because souls came from the Essence like a son from a father: one essence, yet a being of his own. The running is the son's. → (M13) This is why a Jew gives up his life rather than deny G-d: he simply cannot be separated. → (M10) Two faithful friends can lose touch for a while. A father and son's love is never hidden. → (M4) So thinking is not really the cause of the love. It only uncovers what is in the soul's essence. → (M15) The same shows in plain wholeness of deed: a simple Jew just won't sin on purpose. It is like nature, from the same root. → (M15) And in every test: the soul is 'sworn' at birth, which means 'filled', given strength from the essence that is bound to G-d. ⇒ BOTTOM: The soul's pull to G-d is its nature, like a son's; contemplation only uncovers it (stated, A163-174). ⇒ RETURN: When tested, lean on the essence that cannot be separated; keep the plain deed whole (stated, A164). - RK:RK-C07 [A strong spiritual arousal should change a person.] (M1) Many are deeply moved on Rosh Hashana and Yom Kippur, and then go on through the year unchanged. It is truly a wonder. → (M7) Because what was moved is the surrounding love, like faith. It doesn't enter the working powers: 'the thief at the tunnel's mouth calls on G-d'. → (M11) But when the arousal comes from the narrow place, when even the heart's inner point was dressed in the wrong things and it hurts, the breakout is above reason, and it does change the body. → (M5) When the essence of the soul is truly revealed, it turns the animal soul around: someone who came to give his life and was saved becomes 'a different person'. → (M10) This is remarkable, because the animal soul by itself only wants to live. → (M8) Deepest: when the animal soul itself is in pain and has no place in itself, the cry comes from it too. → (M9) Then the cry is like someone running from death to life, and he changes completely. → (M15) It works only where you worked all year. On the High Holy Days the Source comes close to the spark and reveals the essence, but it lands in exactly the things you labored on. → (M15) Whoever toils on Erev Shabbos, in thought and heart, eats on Shabbos, at the level above reason. ⇒ BOTTOM: Only the essence of the soul transforms, and it comes out through the narrow place, on top of daily work (stated, A178-197). ⇒ RETURN: Know your one unrefined trait and work on it all year; then the great days reach it (stated, A187-193). - RK:RK-C08 [Love is higher than fear.] (M9) Some people are born with heaven's yoke on them. They don't look at what is forbidden, simply: 'but one may not!' It takes no effort. → (M1) But no one is born loving G-d. Love needs knowing. → (M2) Because the yoke is not about any detail. It is a feeling of G-dliness as a whole, which is really a hidden sense of the Essence itself, above every level. → (M5) So at every level love belongs to lights and revelations, and fear, even the lowest, belongs to the Essence. → (M2) That is why remembering is enough for fear: self-nullification is natural to Jews. Love reveals something new, so it needs real thinking. → (M16) Everyone can feel, any time, that no place is empty of Him, and that He sees, listens and understands. → (M5) Highest fear: the Infinite Light, as it is in itself, is found here below, openly, as it is. → (M15) That is inner fear: nullified in your very being. ⇒ BOTTOM: The plain 'one may not' touches the Essence, hidden; love touches His lights (stated, A107). ⇒ RETURN: Keep the fear by remembering all day that He stands over you; keep love for prayer, by thinking (stated, A101, A112). - RK:RK-C09 [I understand G-d through examples.] (M17) Even in the outer stage, while explaining it to yourself, keep the sense that you are dealing with something G-dly, not just studying an idea. → (M7) Then strip: put down the pictures you understood it through, and come to the thing itself. → (M4) Because the G-dly thing is not in that shape or picture at all. → (M10) Yet you can't fully strip it. A thin garment stays, because no one can grasp the G-dly with no garment at all. → (M8) A true neshama grasps the G-dly directly. The sign: the natural senses fall still from closeness to the light. → (M6) From the light inside the worlds to the light beyond them: 'to understand a thing from within a thing'. → (M5) This becomes knowing by 'not': knowing what He is not, and the wonder, which is seeing with the mind's eye. → (M15) It ends in the bow of the Amidah: nullified in being, 'its head reaching heaven'. → (M15) And afterwards he 'takes a G-dly matter altogether differently': even his outer understanding changes. ⇒ BOTTOM: Through pictures you reach the thing; at the top you know it by what it is not, and you bow (stated, A42-53). ⇒ RETURN: The plainest start is the truest: 'Modeh Ani', giving your whole self, is nearer to every Jew than any particular (stated, A29). - RK:RK-C10 [To know G-d is to understand Him.] (M9) A gifted child understands everything and still does not feel the thing itself. That is why a child is not yet punished. → (M3) Cruelty is lack of feeling: the cruel man understands the other's poverty but does not feel it. → (M2) Daas is feeling. 'Add knowledge, add pain.' → (M1) If you tie your mind only to the explanation, the heart gets just a leftover sentence, 'G-dliness is good and wonderful', and it fades fast. → (M7) Tie your mind to the G-dly thing itself. Then the explanations become a passageway, and you feel the light. → (M16) Then the heart moves by itself, with no forcing. → (M13) Feeling how high G-dliness is, is feeling how low the bad is. That is where real choice comes from. → (M15) A general resolution doesn't reach the particular. Name the one trait you must fix and aim your prayer at it. → (M15) Not once or twice, but in many prayers on one trait, until it bends. ⇒ BOTTOM: Knowing G-d means feeling Him, and feeling Him is also feeling the bad as bad (stated, T61-80). ⇒ RETURN: Pray at length on one matter, tied to its essence; aim many prayers at your one trait (stated, T57, T104). - RK:RK-C11 [Before praying I need to get ready.] (M9) Clear your mind of worries. You do it every night to fall asleep, so you can do it for your soul. → (M7) Make an honest accounting, at a fitting time, until the heart truly breaks. A broken heart is the vessel for G-d's light. → (M8) If that doesn't work, pity your own soul. Her root's root is the inner Essence of the Infinite, and she came down so low. → (M10) We don't know how great the pity is, because we don't feel how high she was. So we ask 'in Your great mercy'. → (M5) At the Amidah: the Essence of the Source is revealed and found in every single place. → (M4) Hiding and revealing don't apply to Him at all. → (M15) Then great fear falls on you, and an inner stillness. ⇒ BOTTOM: You stand before an Essence that is not hidden anywhere (stated, T91). ⇒ RETURN: Prepare: clear worries, honest accounting at times, mercy on your soul, then stand still before Him (stated, T88-93). - RK:RK-C12 [A person sins only when a spirit of folly enters him.] (M4) After the first sin, people know things by natural feeling, 'nice' and 'ugly', instead of by true understanding. The Rambam calls this a descent. → (M7) Prophets, while prophesying, stripped their clothes: they stripped their natural senses. That is why a prophet was called 'mad'. → (M12) So there is a holy folly. 'Better to be called a fool all my life than be wicked for one hour.' → (M5) Holy folly doesn't calculate at all, not even holy calculations. It acts from the strength of the soul above reason. → (M16) If your soul's light isn't shining and you are caught, this is your counsel: don't argue in detail. Stand against every obstacle with great strength. → (M11) 'For it is a stiff-necked people, so forgive': the stiff neck is a virtue, the soul's strength to do good with no reason and no debate. → (M9) A stubborn man's will is stronger than a reasonable man's, because reason weakens and limits will. → (M10) A river is wide but can dry up. A spring drips drop by drop and never stops. The spring is the soul's wisdom joined to its root. → (M15) So do the opposite of your yetzer, like a lion, with no argument and no calculation, in the very thing you are sunk in. → (M15) After acting this way for a while, holy understanding comes back, and you can know Him in all your ways. ⇒ BOTTOM: Under reason is a strength that needs no reason: the spring of the soul (stated, U556-571). ⇒ RETURN: Where you are stuck, don't negotiate: do the opposite, steadily; understanding follows (stated, U585-586). - RK:RK-C13 [The yetzer says doing this wrong won't cut you off.] (M1) The yetzer's deepest lie: 'Even if you do this bad thing, you are not separated from G-d at all.' → (M11) It works because the yetzer knows the truth about us: a Jew cannot bear to be separated. If he knew this act separates, he would never do it. → (M2) The root of idol worship is not denying G-d. It is thinking of yourself as a thing on your own, separate from Him. → (M10) Even the forces of evil know their Master and do not rebel against Him. → (M5) So 'the gnat came before you': in the moment of sin a person is further from holiness than the lowest creature, which keeps its Maker's command. → (M3) The soul is tied to G-d by a rope made of 613 thin strands. Each sin cuts a strand and weakens the whole rope. ⇒ BOTTOM: The yetzer uses your inseparability against you; seeing the truth stops it (stated, U39, U77). ⇒ RETURN: When tempted, see plainly: this act does separate; the hidden love will then refuse it (stated, U77). - RK:RK-C14 [The soul came down into the body.] (M6) The light that gives life to the worlds always runs back to its source, like fire rising up, because everything wants to be included in its root. → (M14) Above, the soul had nothing in its way and knew nothing of the opposite, so it had no work to do. → (M11) This thirst did not exist in the soul before. It is born only from coming down into the body. → (M10) Now the thirst can't be quenched, because now we know only that G-d exists, not what He is. → (M10) The animal soul is 'quenched': its wants are cheap and always near. 'No one is richer than the pig', whose food is garbage. → (M15) Yet G-d's laughter, His joy, comes exactly from service in this world of 'somethings': 'G-d made laughter for me'. ⇒ BOTTOM: Thirst for G-d is born of descent and cannot be quenched here; that is its gift (stated, U109-118). ⇒ RETURN: Let the body's needs be for heaven's sake; the joy above comes from that (stated, U108-109). - RK:RK-C15 [Man was made from the lowest dust.] (M2) The higher it is, the lower it falls. → (M9) A stone from the bottom of a wall falls close by. A stone from the top falls far away. → (M14) Man's soul is the highest, so he got the coarsest body, because he has the power to refine the coarsest stuff. → (M11) That is why some people are crueler than wild beasts, 'making it tight and bad for the other'. → (M5) But a person in this coarse world who grasps G-dliness and longs for Him is higher than the angels, because he lifts up the physical world. → (M15) The purpose: to turn the darkness of the coarse body itself into light. ⇒ BOTTOM: Your coarseness is the sign of how high your soul is (stated, U305-306). ⇒ RETURN: Don't despair of your body or nature; it is the material you were sent to turn into light (stated, U309, U312). - RK:RK-C16 [The scholar's prayer is greater than the simple person's.] (M9) The simple Jew does what he can, in speech and deed, plainly and wholly. → (M4) He doesn't understand his Tehillim, so he gets no pleasure from it. For him it is pure acceptance of the yoke. → (M8) He feels very low and crushed that he knows so little. → (M15) And still he says Tehillim aloud and sweetly. → (M5) Acceptance of the yoke is the highest service and the foundation of all the rest. → (M11) A scholar who takes credit shows he is serving himself, like any other pleasure. → (M13) Nothing is yours: 'give Him what is His, for you and yours are His'. → (M12) A sinner at least knows he did what one may not do, and it bothers him. That is already a movement of teshuva. Pride doesn't even know it is lacking. ⇒ BOTTOM: The simple, unrewarded 'yes' to G-d is higher than enjoyed understanding (stated, U320-330). ⇒ RETURN: Say your Tehillim even when you don't understand; never take credit for understanding (stated, U319-336). - RK:RK-C17 [Why do people who don't keep the Torah seem to prosper?] (M4) The Infinite is not affected by what creatures do: 'I, G-d, have not changed', from before creation to after. → (M9) Will is not in any one organ. It is in the foot just as in the head. → (M9) When you want to move your foot, it moves at once, with no delay; a dance step learned by the mind takes time. → (M2) Light through channels needs a vessel, and the vessel is self-nullification. → (M11) 'I and he cannot live together' with the proud. → (M10) But the surrounding light needs no vessel, so it reaches even where it isn't fit, like will reaching the foot. → (M5) Yet Israel's pain reaches the very inside: 'in all their trouble He is troubled'. → (M16) In trouble the inner point shows openly in everyone, as simple faith in G-d who works wonders. → (M2) Truth is a straight line from beginning to end: the hidden inside shows completely, as it is. → (M15) So the cry must come from the depth of the heart; then it reaches the Essence. ⇒ BOTTOM: The Essence is not moved by deeds, yet the inner pain of a Jew reaches it, through a cry from the depth (stated, U216-233). ⇒ RETURN: In trouble, cry out simply and from the depth; the inner point is open then (stated, U229). - RK:RK-C18 [I got excited in prayer, then cooled off when I tried to think in detail.] (M2) General contemplation is only a remembering of G-d. It can excite you, but it is not yet knowing. → (M4) If you cooled when you began to think in detail, don't worry. You weren't really hot. → (M11) Beginners fall down in their own eyes and decide they aren't suited for this. → (M4) In no way is it so. Such a person does not exist. → (M16) When it feels heavy and you 'don't take up anything', enter by accepting the yoke. Then it becomes tasty and comes alive in you. → (M17) With each return to the same matter it gets clearer and becomes united with you. → (M1) It is easier to want high things falsely than to hold a low thing truly. → (M17) Truth comes step by step, and each step is true in itself. → (M15) Find the one thing you really hold onto, that truly touches you, and build from there. ⇒ BOTTOM: Coolness is not failure and unsuitability is not real; truth grows step by step (stated, A226-264). ⇒ RETURN: Pick one matter at your level, return to it again and again, and demand of yourself only what truly touches you (stated, A249-265). - RK:RK-C19 [Who am I to love G-d? That's for great tzaddikim.] (M1) Many say: 'What are we? Love and fear are for others.' When they learn about love, it sounds like something outside them. → (M3) But love and fear are commanded to everyone, and G-d does not demand the impossible. → (M4) Love is already in every soul by nature. It only needs to be brought from hidden to open, each by his own measure. → (M9) Like meeting someone you never knew: when you learn his worth, you love him. → (M8) The love every Jew has: 'my soul, I desire You', like a weak person longing for his life to return to him. → (M12) In fear-and-service, the servant's joy is the Master's joy itself, 'for the servant is like the master himself, one thing with him'. → (M16) Never think a teaching on love doesn't apply to you. → (M17) Pray with what you learned once, twice, three times. → (M4) It is the reverse of what you think: you are low because you lack love, not unworthy of love because you are low. ⇒ BOTTOM: Love is in you already; your lowness comes from not uncovering it (stated, E305-331). ⇒ RETURN: Take what you learned into prayer, repeat it, and begin with a little (stated, E323, E331). - RK:RK-C20 [Love of G-d means feeling warm toward Him.] (M9) Start with life: anyone can feel there is a life in him that gives him life. 'From my flesh I see G-d.' → (M2) Love is moving from place to place. The nearer you come to G-d, the further you are from the coarse. → (M16) After a good prayer one is 'a different person' for hours, even a day. → (M7) For fire-love, after feeling G-d's worth, look at how far you are from Him. → (M5) Water-love only weakens the animal soul. Fire-love burns it and even lifts its natural fire into holiness, which bitterness alone does not do. → (M15) At its height the fire is a thirst to be included in His very Essence; its fuel is your own pleasure and heat, offered up like the fat and blood of a sacrifice (A150). ⇒ BOTTOM: The same passion that pulls to the coarse is lifted, not killed, by fire-love (stated, A150). ⇒ RETURN: In prayer, first feel G-d's worth, then your distance; let the heat rise (stated, A147-157). - RK:RK-C21 [My nature is too hot; I couldn't help it.] (M4) 'My nature is hot' is no excuse: the camel's load matches the camel. A stronger animal soul comes with a stronger G-dly soul. → (M10) The animal soul came first and is the 'old king'. But Jacob was truly the firstborn: in a tube, the first in comes out last. → (M2) Before birth the soul is 'sworn', meaning 'filled': given strength from its ancient root. → (M5) An oath works because hidden essential powers face no obstacle. ⇒ BOTTOM: Your G-dly soul's root is older than your nature's root (stated, U272-285). ⇒ RETURN: Don't blame nature or friends; wake the strength you were given and choose (stated, U286-295). - RK:RK-C22 [Prayer is asking.] (M2) Prayer is pouring out the soul: flames of longing and thirst to be included in His Essence. → (M10) The letters of Torah come from above like stones G-d made. The letters of prayer are bricks made by man, words from the depth of the heart, the nearness after distance. → (M15) The words you used all day, refined, become shining stones, a crown for your Maker. ⇒ BOTTOM: Your daily speech, gathered into prayer, becomes a crown (stated, A60). ⇒ RETURN: Pray with your own heart's words, even after a day far away; the distance is part of the crown.

## RM — the Rashab's maamarim

The Rebbe Rashab's Sefer HaMaamarim (all years, OCR) takes the depth below 'nothing but Him' in four directions. First, the self: the soul is not a ray that vanishes but His essence become someone, like a child from a father; even yechidah is only a name, and under it is the 'true Something'. Second, the bottom: under understanding and under will is 'the deep that cannot be blocked'; the deepest cry has no sound; the deepest bond is a knot, not a melting, and it shows in the plain mitzvah. Third, the person who feels nothing: the distance itself is a door, and a 'something' that becomes 'nothing' is higher than a born nothing. Fourth, the return: the deepest run already holds the return; joy is 'His will got done'; and He is not 'all things merged' but the Only One, einzig, who carries all and freely chooses us.

## Depths

- **RM:RM-D1 Even 'yechidah' is a name** (extends Extends D6 ('under all your names'): the Rashab goes one step further, below yechidah itself.): Under nefesh, ruach, neshamah, chayah and even yechidah is the soul's essence. The Sages say 'five names' were given to the soul; yechidah is one of them, the 'nothing' of the true Something. Your essence is the true Something itself (stated). | Inside: Like light that vanishes at sunset: what you thought was you was the glow; what guards it is under every name (stated image). | Lands: Body: stillness behind the breath. Now: 'You guard it within me'. Deed: teshuvah comes from here (7761). | Q: חמשה שמות נקראו לה נר"ן חי' יחידה — five names were given to it (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:7759)
- **RM:RM-D2 The essence became you** (extends Sharpens D1 (given, not erased) and D11 (called into being): adds why: you are of the essence, not of its glow.): You are not a ray that vanishes before the sun. A soul comes the way a child comes from a father: the essence itself becomes a someone. That is why you stay real even before His essence, and do not change in any world (stated). | Inside: Not crushed by 'nothing but Him': held, as a child is held (ours, on stated lines). | Lands: Body: a steady weight in the chair. Now: 'in every world unchanged'. Deed: you can draw Him down with Torah and mitzvos (35572). | Q: שהעצם גופא נעשה בבחי' מהות ומציאות דבר — the essence itself becomes a real being (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:35552)
- **RM:RM-D3 The deep that cannot be blocked** (extends Extends D8 (the bond) and D9 (the bottom sends you back): the bottom is a source, not just a floor.): Your understanding can be blocked by sin. Your will above reason can stop. The yechidah, 'the springs of the great deep', cannot; from there everything missing refills, and you are wanted more than before (stated). | Inside: Like water breaking up from the deep and filling every dry bed (stated image). | Lands: Body: breath from the belly, lower than words. Now: 'in one hour'. Deed: the next mitzvah, from there. | Q: יש מעיינות שאינם נפסקים לעולם והם הנק' מעיינות תהום רבה — springs that never stop, the springs of the great deep (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:7784)
- **RM:RM-D4 The silent cry** (extends New (between D2 and D8): a cry that is not effort and not feeling.): Past the loud cry of distress is the heart's own cry, too deep to make a sound, where the self is moved without feeling itself. It is a flame melting into the great fire with no noise. The shofar takes this quiet breath and makes it a great voice (stated). | Inside: From inside: quiet, not empty; moved, not excited (stated). | Lands: Body: one quiet exhale. Now: this breath. Deed: hear the shofar; walk 'in the light of Your face'. | Q: עד שלא יוכל לצעוק בקול כלל — until he cannot cry aloud at all (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:7778)
- **RM:RM-D5 Two nothings** (extends Extends D3 (even with Him, nothing) and D4 (neither something nor nothing) with the Rashab's proof from the creature's own existence.): There are two 'nothings'. One is His light lost in Him, so lost no name fits it. The other is the Source of creatures, called 'nothing' only because we cannot grasp it; yet it truly exists. Your very existence proves both: you come from 'not a thing', and that Not-a-thing is real (stated). | Inside: Clear and cool, like counting back to a start (ours). | Lands: Body: feel your own limits (skin, edges) as proof of a Source. Now: this edge. Deed: thank Him for being here. | Q: נמצא שהנבראים במה שהם נמצאים במציאות יש, ה'"ה מכריחים שהמקור הוא בחי' אין ולא דבר — the creatures force us to say the source is nothing, not a thing (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:14846)
- **RM:RM-D6 A knot, not a melting** (extends Sharpens D8 (the bond): bittul is chayah; bond is yechidah.): At the top of seeing (chayah) a person drops his self like stone before the king. Deeper, the yechidah does not drop the self; it is tied: 'with one knot I am knotted to Him'. And that knot shows in the plainest place, the mitzvah in deed (stated). | Inside: Tied, not dissolved; like a candle held in a torch (27956). | Lands: Body: hands. Now: the deed in front of you. Deed: the mitzvah. | Q: ולא בבחי ביטול רק בבחי' דביקות הנפש שדבוק בעצמי' אוא"ס — not nullification but cleaving (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:27956)
- **RM:RM-D7 Feeling nothing is a door** (extends New: a route to the bottom for those with no light at all; deepens D9.): Some people feel nothing, even in the holiest days. The Rashab does not tell them to feel. Their turning comes from the narrowness itself, like one running from death. A something that becomes nothing is higher than a born nothing (stated). | Inside: Narrow, then broken open; not warm (stated). | Lands: Body: the chest. Now: this narrowness. Deed: one small careful act. | Q: כ'א מפני שצר לו מעוצם הריחוק שלו — only from the pain of his distance (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:40388)
- **RM:RM-D8 His pleasure is my pleasure** (extends Extends D12 (deed) and D13 (plainness): the delight inside the plain deed.): The servant who gave himself away polishes the work because the master's pleasure is his. That joy, 'that His will got done', is deeper than any felt love of delight (stated). | Inside: Quiet, steady pleasure, not a high (stated). | Lands: Body: hands, finished work. Now: done. Deed: the mitzvah. | Q: ולזאת הנח"ר של האדון הוא הנח"ר שלו — the master's pleasure is his pleasure (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:21749)
- **RM:RM-D9 Only One, carrying all** (extends Extends D4 and ON's ladder with the Rashab's 'einzig'.): The deepest unity is not that everything is one in Him. He is single, 'einzig', and carries everything. The four praises climb to 'who is like You': His essence (stated). | Inside: Spacious; carried rather than dissolved (ours). | Lands: Body: the floor holding you. Now: carried. Deed: Shema. | Q: לא שכולם בו בבחי' אחדות, כ"א הוא בבחי' יחיד ממש, איינציג בעצם, שנושא את הכל — not all in Him as one; He is single, einzig, carrying all (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:14484)
- **RM:RM-D10 You cannot measure your own depth** (extends New guard against spiritual pride; pairs with D14 (no bottom).): Being good on the outside does not prove you reached the depth. Rabban Yochanan ben Zakai wept; Rebbi wept at a sinner's one hour. So 'be in your own eyes like a rasha', not to despair but to keep gathering yourself (stated). | Inside: Honest, a little shaky, and free (ours). | Lands: Body: lower the shoulders. Now: unknown. Deed: pray. | Q: לא ידעתי את נפשי — I do not know my soul (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:498)
- **RM:RM-D11 The bond with no feeling** (extends Sharpens D8: the bond itself is felt as no-feeling.): Real self-sacrifice has no delight and no seeking, only the bond. That want for G-d has no cause, shows by itself or when something opposes it, and never changes (stated). | Inside: Flat and solid, not emotional (stated). | Lands: Body: the spine. Now: unchanged. Deed: keep the mitzvah even when dry. | Q: דבמסינ בפועל אין בזה שום הרגש עונג, ולא שום מבוקש כלל וכלל, כ"א מצד עצם ההתקשרות — no delight and nothing sought, only the bond (Chassidus-txt/R. Shalom Dovber Schneersohn (Rebbe Rashab) (1860-1920)/Sefer HaMaamarim (scans, OCR).txt:30411)

## Chains (start → steps → bottom → return)

- RM:RM-C01 [Something in me is blocked. I did wrong, and the feeling for G-d dried up.] (M11) When you do wrong, the river inside you can dry up. Your mind and heart go dull, like a river whose spring got blocked. → (M6) The fix is not to clean the old opening. You dig deeper than the place the water first came out. → (M1) But even a deeper spring can get blocked again. A strong burst of 'I want G-d', above reason, can still fade. → (M5) There are springs that never stop: the springs of the great deep. They come from the deep itself, not from cracks in the ground. → (M8) In you that deep is the yechidah, the soul's single point. There, nothing can block or stop. → (M15) From there everything missing fills again, even mitzvos you never did. One hour can 'buy a world'. → (M4) So after a real return He is not just willing again. He is more pleased than before the sin. ⇒ BOTTOM: The yechidah: the deep under the deep, where no sin and no dullness can stop the flow (stated). ⇒ RETURN: Every river refills from there: mind, heart and deeds; this is why Rosh Hashanah's work is teshuvah from the heart's point, and the year's work is the mitzvos it then fills (stated; 'the whole river' image is ours). - RM:RM-C02 [I want to cry out to G-d, and I can't even find words.] (M2) There is a cry with no words. It comes when the feeling is too big for the mind and heart to hold. → (M4) But a loud cry still feels itself. Someone is still there, shouting. → (M5) Deeper is the heart's own cry. It goes so deep it cannot even make a sound. → (M8) Then the whole self is moved from its place, without feeling itself at all. → (M10) Picture a spark racing to a flame: still a spark. Then picture a flame melting into the great fire: gone, with no noise. → (M7) That silent cry is the shofar. A small breath goes in at the narrow end, and the shofar makes it great. → (M15) Then you walk 'in the light of Your face': face turned to face, and new life flows into the year. ⇒ BOTTOM: A cry with no sound and no self-feeling, from the yechidah (stated). ⇒ RETURN: The Rashab says this stirring often comes from above, 'when the Source is near' in the Ten Days; and the shofar brings it out as a voice, then into the year (stated). - RM:RM-C03 [Who am I, really, under all of it?] (M3) Each morning you say: 'the soul You gave me is pure.' Those words list four levels of you. → (M2) But 'You gave' is more than 'You breathed'. Giving means giving with an open hand, the essence itself. → (M6) That essence guards all the rest, the way the sun guards its light. When the sun sets, the light is gone at once. → (M4) Even 'yechidah' is only a name. The Sages say the soul has five names. → (M5) Yechidah is the 'nothing' of the True Something. Your real self is the True Something itself. → (M6) And this did not come by creation. G-dliness itself spread out and became a soul. ⇒ BOTTOM: Under every name, even yechidah, is the soul's essence, the true Something, one with His (stated). ⇒ RETURN: From there comes teshuvah, 'the spirit returns to G-d who gave it' (7761): the bottom is where the turning begins (stated). - RM:RM-C04 [If everything is nothing before Him, am I nothing too?] (M3) A teacher can only give you light. If you have no mind of your own, his teaching will not take. → (M10) A father gives himself. From the drop, a whole new person comes, with a mind of his own. → (M2) So in begetting, the essence itself becomes a 'someone'. → (M4) Creatures are not like that. If their source shone on them, they would vanish. Even now they are not truly real. → (M12) Souls are the opposite. A soul can take in even the essence's own light and not disappear. → (M13) A soul stays the same in every world it goes down to. An essence does not run out or stop. → (M14) That G-d Himself should be a 'someone' is so hard that the Zohar calls it birth pains. ⇒ BOTTOM: You are not a ray that is about to vanish. You are His essence become someone (stated). ⇒ RETURN: So only souls can bring His essence down, through Torah and mitzvos (35572); and they cannot be ruined: like a nut fallen into filth, washed and still good (35569) (stated). - RM:RM-C05 [People say the world is 'nothing' before G-d. What kind of nothing?] (M3) There are two 'knowings'. From above: He is the something, and all is nothing. From below: we are something, and He is the nothing we came from. → (M2) The first nothing: His light inside Him is so lost in Him that no name can be put on it at all. → (M4) The second nothing is different. We call our Source 'nothing' only because we cannot grasp it. → (M14) Your being here proves your Source is 'not a thing'.
